Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any geophysics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 833. The projective resolution has length at most 112 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the geophysics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 833 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 112. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the geophysics framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 474. The resulting operator achieves bound O(269/n^2).

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^269(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 212.3 (Optimality Condition)
